Appendix A - Equipment Deep Dive Cards
Use these cards for deeper revision. Each card gives you the purpose, safety points, cleaning points and a dish example. This helps you answer follow-up questions instead of memorising only one short answer.
Stove
Purpose: Used for boiling, simmering, pan-frying, sauteing and making sauces.
Safety: Keep pan handles turned inwards, control flame, avoid loose clothing and report gas smell immediately.
Cleaning / maintenance: Turn off after use, wipe spills, clean burners or surface when cool and keep area dry.
Example dish / task: Boiling pasta, making soup, pan-frying chicken.
Dịch: Bếp dùng để luộc, nấu lăn tăn, áp chảo và làm sốt. Cần kiểm soát lửa, quay cán chảo vào trong và vệ sinh sau khi nguội.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for boiling, simmering, pan-frying, sauteing and making sauces.
What is the main hazard?
Keep pan handles turned inwards, control flame, avoid loose clothing and report gas s
How do you clean it?
Turn off after use, wipe spills, clean burners or surface when cool and keep area dry

Flat Top Grill
Purpose: Used for steaks, burgers, eggs, vegetables and breakfast items.
Safety: Use scraper safely, keep hands away from hot surface, use dry cloths and tongs.
Cleaning / maintenance: Scrape food residue, wipe grease, clean with approved method and oil lightly if required.
Example dish / task: Grilled steak, burger patties, bacon and eggs.
Dịch: Flat top dùng để áp/nướng steak, burger, trứng và rau. Cần tránh bỏng và vệ sinh dầu mỡ sau service.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for steaks, burgers, eggs, vegetables and breakfast items.
What is the main hazard?
Use scraper safely, keep hands away from hot surface, use dry cloths and tongs.
How do you clean it?
Scrape food residue, wipe grease, clean with approved method and oil lightly if requi


Char Grill
Purpose: Used to grill meat, seafood and vegetables with grill marks and smoky flavour.
Safety: Control flare-ups, use tongs, do not leave food unattended and keep ventilation on.
Cleaning / maintenance: Brush grill bars, remove carbon build-up and clean drip tray.
Example dish / task: Grilled chicken, prawns, lamb cutlets.
Dịch: Char grill dùng để nướng tạo vệt và mùi khói. Cần kiểm soát lửa bùng và vệ sinh vỉ nướng.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used to grill meat, seafood and vegetables with grill marks and smoky flavour.
What is the main hazard?
Control flare-ups, use tongs, do not leave food unattended and keep ventilation on.
How do you clean it?
Brush grill bars, remove carbon build-up and clean drip tray.

Convection Oven
Purpose: Used for baking, roasting and even heat circulation.
Safety: Preheat, use oven gloves, avoid steam/heat when opening door.
Cleaning / maintenance: Cool down, wipe spills, clean trays and report temperature issues.
Example dish / task: Roast vegetables, baked fish, roast chicken.
Dịch: Lò đối lưu dùng quạt để nhiệt đều. Cần làm nóng trước, tránh bỏng và vệ sinh khay.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for baking, roasting and even heat circulation.
What is the main hazard?
Preheat, use oven gloves, avoid steam/heat when opening door.
How do you clean it?
Cool down, wipe spills, clean trays and report temperature issues.


Combi Oven
Purpose: Used with steam, dry heat or combination for roasting, steaming, baking and reheating.
Safety: Open door slowly to avoid steam burns, use correct program and check probe temperature.
Cleaning / maintenance: Run cleaning cycle if available, wipe door seals, clean trays and report faults.
Example dish / task: Steam vegetables, roast chicken, reheat banquet food.
Dịch: Lò combi dùng hơi nước và nhiệt khô. Cần cẩn thận bỏng hơi và chọn chương trình đúng.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used with steam, dry heat or combination for roasting, steaming, baking and reheating
What is the main hazard?
Open door slowly to avoid steam burns, use correct program and check probe temperatur
How do you clean it?
Run cleaning cycle if available, wipe door seals, clean trays and report faults.

Salamander
Purpose: Used for browning, melting cheese, glazing and finishing dishes from above.
Safety: Food burns quickly, so monitor constantly; use tongs and heat-proof gloves.
Cleaning / maintenance: Allow to cool, clean tray, remove burnt residue and check controls.
Example dish / task: Gratin, melted cheese, finishing fish.
Dịch: Salamander làm vàng mặt trên, chảy phô mai và hoàn thiện món nhanh. Phải theo dõi vì rất dễ cháy.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for browning, melting cheese, glazing and finishing dishes from above.
What is the main hazard?
Food burns quickly, so monitor constantly; use tongs and heat-proof gloves.
How do you clean it?
Allow to cool, clean tray, remove burnt residue and check controls.


Deep Fryer
Purpose: Used for deep-frying chips, chicken, calamari and battered items.
Safety: Do not overfill, keep water away, lower basket slowly and watch for oil splashes.
Cleaning / maintenance: Filter or change oil, clean basket and well, dispose of oil correctly.
Example dish / task: Fish and chips, crumbed chicken, spring rolls.
Dịch: Nồi chiên dùng cho món chiên ngập dầu. Nguy hiểm chính là dầu nóng, nước và cháy dầu.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for deep-frying chips, chicken, calamari and battered items.
What is the main hazard?
Do not overfill, keep water away, lower basket slowly and watch for oil splashes.
How do you clean it?
Filter or change oil, clean basket and well, dispose of oil correctly.

Bain Marie
Purpose: Used to keep cooked food hot during service.
Safety: Keep hot food above 60C, do not mix new and old food, avoid dry-out.
Cleaning / maintenance: Empty safely, clean water pan, sanitise food containers and dry.
Example dish / task: Keeping soups, sauces or curry hot.
Dịch: Bain marie giữ nóng thức ăn. Cần duy trì trên 60C và không trộn thức ăn mới/cũ.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used to keep cooked food hot during service.
What is the main hazard?
Keep hot food above 60C, do not mix new and old food, avoid dry-out.
How do you clean it?
Empty safely, clean water pan, sanitise food containers and dry.


Meat Slicer
Purpose: Used for slicing cooked meat, ham, cheese or cold cuts evenly.
Safety: Use safety guard and pusher, keep hands away from blade, unplug before cleaning.
Cleaning / maintenance: Set blade to zero, unplug, clean blade carefully and sanitise food-contact parts.
Example dish / task: Slicing ham for sandwiches or cooked beef.
Dịch: Máy thái thịt rất nguy hiểm vì lưỡi sắc. Phải dùng guard, pusher và rút điện khi vệ sinh.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for slicing cooked meat, ham, cheese or cold cuts evenly.
What is the main hazard?
Use safety guard and pusher, keep hands away from blade, unplug before cleaning.
How do you clean it?
Set blade to zero, unplug, clean blade carefully and sanitise food-contact parts.

Food Processor
Purpose: Used for chopping, slicing, grating and pureeing ingredients quickly.
Safety: Use the pusher, never fingers; keep lid locked; avoid overloading.
Cleaning / maintenance: Unplug, remove blade carefully, wash, sanitise and air dry.
Example dish / task: Chopping herbs, making pesto, slicing vegetables.
Dịch: Food processor giúp cắt/xay nhanh. Nguy hiểm chính là lưỡi dao và máy chạy khi chưa khóa nắp.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for chopping, slicing, grating and pureeing ingredients quickly.
What is the main hazard?
Use the pusher, never fingers; keep lid locked; avoid overloading.
How do you clean it?
Unplug, remove blade carefully, wash, sanitise and air dry.


Stick Blender
Purpose: Used for blending soups, sauces and purees directly in pots or containers.
Safety: Keep blade fully submerged to reduce splashing; be careful with hot liquids.
Cleaning / maintenance: Unplug, wash shaft/blade, sanitise and dry before storage.
Example dish / task: Pumpkin soup, tomato sauce, puree.
Dịch: Máy xay cầm tay dùng xay súp/sốt. Cần tránh bắn chất lỏng nóng và vệ sinh lưỡi.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for blending soups, sauces and purees directly in pots or containers.
What is the main hazard?
Keep blade fully submerged to reduce splashing; be careful with hot liquids.
How do you clean it?
Unplug, wash shaft/blade, sanitise and dry before storage.

Stand Mixer
Purpose: Used for dough, batter, whipped cream and pastry preparation.
Safety: Use correct attachment, keep guard closed and never put hands in while running.
Cleaning / maintenance: Turn off, unplug if needed, remove bowl/attachment and clean properly.
Example dish / task: Pizza dough, whipped cream, cake batter.
Dịch: Mixer dùng nhào/đánh. Không đưa tay vào khi máy chạy và dùng đúng attachment.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for dough, batter, whipped cream and pastry preparation.
What is the main hazard?
Use correct attachment, keep guard closed and never put hands in while running.
How do you clean it?
Turn off, unplug if needed, remove bowl/attachment and clean properly.


Sous Vide Machine
Purpose: Used for controlled low-temperature cooking in vacuum bags.
Safety: Use correct time and temperature, seal bags properly and chill safely if not serving immediately.
Cleaning / maintenance: Clean water bath, wipe machine and keep bags labelled.
Example dish / task: Sous vide chicken breast, steak, eggs.
Dịch: Sous vide nấu nhiệt thấp kiểm soát. Cần đúng thời gian/nhiệt độ và ghi nhãn túi.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for controlled low-temperature cooking in vacuum bags.
What is the main hazard?
Use correct time and temperature, seal bags properly and chill safely if not serving 
How do you clean it?
Clean water bath, wipe machine and keep bags labelled.

Dishwasher
Purpose: Used for washing and sanitising plates, utensils and some tools.
Safety: Load correctly, avoid overcrowding and check chemical levels/cycle temperature.
Cleaning / maintenance: Clean filters, wipe seals and leave door open if required to dry.
Example dish / task: Washing plates, cutlery, kitchen tools.
Dịch: Máy rửa chén rửa và khử khuẩn. Cần gạt thức ăn thừa, xếp đúng và kiểm tra đồ sạch.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for washing and sanitising plates, utensils and some tools.
What is the main hazard?
Load correctly, avoid overcrowding and check chemical levels/cycle temperature.
How do you clean it?
Clean filters, wipe seals and leave door open if required to dry.


Cool Room
Purpose: Used for safe chilled storage of perishable food.
Safety: Keep 0-5C, food off floor, raw food below cooked food and door closed.
Cleaning / maintenance: Clean shelves, check labels, remove expired food and record temperature.
Example dish / task: Storing meat, dairy, prepared items.
Dịch: Kho mát giữ thực phẩm lạnh. Cần sắp xếp đúng kệ, FIFO và ghi nhiệt độ.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for safe chilled storage of perishable food.
What is the main hazard?
Keep 0-5C, food off floor, raw food below cooked food and door closed.
How do you clean it?
Clean shelves, check labels, remove expired food and record temperature.

Freezer
Purpose: Used for frozen food storage at -18C or below.
Safety: Do not overload, keep packaging sealed and avoid thaw/refreeze.
Cleaning / maintenance: Defrost if required, clean spills and record temperature.
Example dish / task: Frozen seafood, meat, vegetables.
Dịch: Tủ đông bảo quản đông lạnh. Cần -18C hoặc thấp hơn, bao bì kín và không rã đông rồi đông lại.
Interview prompt
Strong response keyword
What is it used for?
Used for frozen food storage at -18C or below.
What is the main hazard?
Do not overload, keep packaging sealed and avoid thaw/refreeze.
How do you clean it?
Defrost if required, clean spills and record temperature.


Appendix B - Knife and Cut Practice Cards
These cards help you practise explaining knife skills with examples. For the interview, you should connect the cut to a real dish or task.
Julienne
Description: Long thin matchstick strips.
Common use: Used for stir-fry vegetables, salad garnish and spring rolls.
Safety / quality point: Keep pieces even so they cook evenly and look professional.
Dịch: Julienne là thái sợi dài mỏng, dùng cho món xào, salad và trang trí.
Brunoise
Description: Very small dice, usually from julienne strips.
Common use: Used for fine garnish, soup garnish and delicate sauces.
Safety / quality point: Requires sharp knife, stable board and careful claw grip.
Dịch: Brunoise là hạt lựu rất nhỏ, cần dao sắc và kỹ thuật tốt.
Macedoine
Description: Medium dice, larger than brunoise.
Common use: Used for salads, soups and vegetable side dishes.
Safety / quality point: Keep size consistent for even cooking.
Dịch: Macedoine là hạt lựu vừa, dùng trong salad và súp.
Batonnet
Description: Thick stick cut.
Common use: Used for chips, vegetable sticks and preparation for dice.
Safety / quality point: Square off the vegetable first for accuracy.
Dịch: Batonnet là cắt dạng thanh, dùng cho khoai chiên hoặc rau củ que.

Chiffonade
Description: Thin ribbons from leafy vegetables or herbs.
Common use: Used for basil, mint, spinach and salad garnish.
Safety / quality point: Stack leaves, roll tightly and slice thinly.
Dịch: Chiffonade là thái chỉ lá rau/thảo mộc.
Paysanne
Description: Thin flat pieces, often square, triangle or round.
Common use: Used for soups and quick-cooking vegetables.
Safety / quality point: Cut evenly so pieces cook at the same speed.
Dịch: Paysanne là miếng mỏng, thường dùng cho súp.
Mirepoix
Description: Rough cut onion, carrot and celery used as aromatic base.
Common use: Used for stocks, soups, sauces and braises.
Safety / quality point: It does not need to be perfectly small because it is usually strained out.
Dịch: Mirepoix là rau củ nền cho nước dùng và sốt.
Dice
Description: Small, medium or large cubes.
Common use: Used for stews, soups, salads and mise en place.
Safety / quality point: Choose the dice size according to cooking time and presentation.
Dịch: Dice là cắt hạt lựu nhiều kích cỡ.

Chapter 1 - Ingredient Safety Foundations
In a Skills Assessment interview, questions about ingredients are not only about cooking. The assessor also checks if you understand receiving, storage, temperature control, cross contamination, personal hygiene, cooking methods and presentation. This chapter gives you the core pattern for answering any ingredient question.
The 6-step answer formula
1. Receiving: check temperature, quality, packaging, date and smell. 2. Storage: keep at the correct temperature, covered, labelled and separated. 3. Preparation: use clean equipment, correct chopping boards and safe knife skills. 4. Cooking: choose the right method and check doneness or internal temperature. 5. Presentation: follow the Standard Recipe Card and use suitable garnish and sauce. 6. Food safety: prevent cross contamination and follow FIFO/HACCP.
Core temperature and storage guide
Item
Temperature
Interview Note
Fresh meat
0-5 C
Store covered, labelled and below ready-to-eat food.
Fresh poultry
0-5 C
Highest risk. Store on the lowest shelf and cook thoroughly.
Fresh seafood
0-5 C
Keep cold, preferably on ice or in the cool room.
Frozen meat/seafood
-18 C or below
Keep hard frozen. Do not refreeze after unsafe thawing.
Hot holding
Above 60 C
Use a bain marie only for hot holding, not cooking.
Danger zone
5-60 C
Minimise time in this range.
Poultry/minced/rolled meats
75 C in the centre
Use a probe thermometer.
Receiving checklist
Check invoice, supplier name and quantity.
Check temperature with a clean thermometer.
Check packaging: no tears, leakage, swollen packs or broken vacuum seal.
Check use-by date, label and product name.
Check quality indicators: colour, smell, texture and freshness.
Reject unsafe or poor-quality products and report to the supervisor.
Common language for interview answers
English expression
Vietnamese meaning
I check the temperature, packaging and use-by date.
Toi kiem tra nhiet do, bao bi va han su dung.
I store raw meat on the lowest shelf.
Toi bao quan thit song o ngan thap nhat.
I prevent cross contamination by separating raw and cooked food.
Toi ngan nhiem cheo bang cach tach thuc pham song va chin.
I follow the Standard Recipe Card for portion size and presentation.
Toi theo cong thuc chuan ve khau phan va trinh bay.
I use a probe thermometer to check the internal temperature.
Toi dung nhiet ke xuyen tam de kiem tra nhiet do ben trong.

Chapter 2 - Meat Master: Beef, Lamb and Pork
Meat questions are common in Cook Skills Assessment because they show your knowledge of cuts, cooking methods, doneness, storage and waste control. Keep answers simple but practical.

Question M1: How do you choose fresh beef?
Model Answer
I check the colour, smell, texture, packaging, use-by date and temperature. Fresh beef should have a natural bright red colour, a clean smell and firm texture. If it smells sour or feels slimy, I reject it and report to my supervisor.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi kiem tra mau sac, mui, ket cau, bao bi, han su dung va nhiet do. Thit bo tuoi co mau do tu nhien, mui sach va ket cau san chac. Neu co mui chua hoac nhot, toi tu choi va bao quan ly.

Question M2: How do you store raw meat?
Model Answer
I store raw meat covered, labelled and dated in the cool room at 0-5 C. I keep it below cooked or ready-to-eat food to prevent meat juices from dripping onto other food. I also follow FIFO.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi bao quan thit song duoc boc kin, dan nhan va ghi ngay trong kho mat 0-5 C. Toi de no ben duoi thuc pham chin de tranh nuoc thit nho xuong.

Question M3: How do you cook a steak medium?
Model Answer
I season the steak, preheat the grill or pan, cook both sides, rest the meat, and check doneness. For medium, I usually aim for about 60-63 C internal temperature and a warm pink centre.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi uop gia vi, lam nong chao hoac bep nuong, nau hai mat, de thit nghi va kiem tra do chin. Medium thuong khoang 60-63 C va ben trong hong am.

Question M4: Why do you rest meat after cooking?
Model Answer
Resting allows the juices to redistribute inside the meat. This makes the meat more tender and juicy and prevents too much juice from running out when sliced.
Dich tieng Viet
De thit nghi giup nuoc thit phan bo deu ben trong, lam thit mem va mong nuoc hon.

Question M6: Which cooking method is suitable for tough meat cuts?
Model Answer

Chapter 3 - Poultry Master: Chicken, Duck, Turkey and Quail
Poultry is a high-risk food. In interviews, you should always mention receiving temperature, storage, thawing, cross contamination and safe cooking temperature.
Common poultry types
Poultry
Methods
Notes
Chicken
Roast, grill, stir-fry, deep-fry, braise
Cook to at least 75 C in the centre.
Duck
Roast, confit, braise
Higher fat content; render fat properly.
Turkey
Roast, braise
Larger bird, longer cooking time.
Quail
Pan-fry, grill, roast
Small bird, cooks quickly.
Chicken parts
Part
Suitable cooking method
Interview note
Breast
Pan-fry, grill, poach
Lean; avoid overcooking.
Thigh
Grill, braise, roast
More flavour and moisture.
Drumstick
Roast, braise, deep-fry
Good for slow cooking or frying.
Wing
Deep-fry, roast
Good for appetisers.
Whole chicken
Roast, poach
Check thickest part with probe thermometer.
Question P1: How do you receive fresh chicken?
Model Answer
I check the invoice, temperature, packaging, use-by date, colour, smell and quantity. Fresh chicken should be cold, well packaged and have no bad smell. If the temperature is too high or the packaging is damaged, I reject it.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi kiem tra hoa don, nhiet do, bao bi, han su dung, mau sac, mui va so luong. Ga tuoi phai lanh, dong goi tot va khong co mui hoi. Neu nhiet do qua cao hoac bao bi hong, toi tu choi.

Question P3: How do you thaw frozen chicken safely?
Model Answer
I thaw frozen chicken in the cool room overnight. I keep it in a container so the juices cannot contaminate other food. I never thaw chicken at room temperature.
Dich tieng Viet

Question P4: What temperature do you cook poultry to?
Model Answer
I cook poultry to at least 75 C in the centre. I check the thickest part using a clean probe thermometer, and the juices should run clear with no pink meat.

Question P5: How do you crumb chicken?
Model Answer
I cut the chicken to the correct size, dry it, season it, coat it in flour, dip it into beaten egg, cover it with breadcrumbs and deep-fry it at about 160-180 C until golden and cooked through.

Chapter 4 - Seafood Master: Fish, Shellfish, Molluscs and Crustaceans
Seafood questions test freshness, storage, knife skills and fast cooking methods. Seafood spoils quickly, so your answers should show strong temperature control and hygiene.
Seafood categories
Category
Examples
Interview note
Round fish
Snapper, salmon, barramundi
Usually yields 2 fillets.
Flat fish
Sole, flounder, halibut
Usually yields 4 fillets.
Crustaceans
Prawns, crab, lobster
Have shells and jointed legs.
Molluscs
Mussels, oysters, clams, scallops
Can be high risk if from polluted water.
Cephalopods
Squid, octopus, calamari
Cook very quickly or slowly.
Fresh fish quality indicators
Indicator
Good quality
Bad sign
Eyes
Clear, bright and slightly bulging
Not cloudy or sunken.
Gills
Bright red or pink
Not brown or slimy.
Skin
Shiny and moist
Scales should be attached.
Flesh
Firm and elastic
Should spring back when pressed.
Smell
Clean sea smell
No strong fishy or ammonia smell.
Fish cuts
Cut
Meaning
Suitable method

Boneless side of fish
Grill, pan-fry, bake
Darne
Thick round slice from round fish
Poach, grill
Supreme
Trimmed fillet portion

Goujon
Small strips
Crumb and deep-fry

Question S1: How do you receive fresh fish?
Model Answer
I check the temperature, smell, eyes, gills, skin, flesh firmness, packaging and use-by date. Fresh fish should have clear eyes, red gills, shiny skin, firm flesh and a clean sea smell.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi kiem tra nhiet do, mui, mat, mang, da, do san chac, bao bi va han su dung. Ca tuoi co mat trong, mang do, da bong, thit san chac va mui bien sach.

Model Answer
I store seafood at 0-5 C, covered and labelled. Fresh seafood is kept cold, usually in the cool room or on ice, and separated from cooked food to prevent cross contamination.

Question S3: How do you fillet a fish?
Model Answer
I place the fish on a stable chopping board, use a sharp filleting knife, cut behind the gills, follow the backbone, remove the fillet, trim it and check for bones.

Toi dat ca tren thot vung, dung dao filet sac, cat sau mang, di doc xuong song, lay filet, cat gon va kiem tra xuong.

Question S5: How do you cook mussels?
Model Answer
I clean and debeard the mussels, discard any broken or open mussels that do not close, then steam them with aromatics until they open. I discard any mussels that remain closed after cooking.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi lam sach va lay rau mussel, bo con vo vo hoac mo khong dong, hap voi huong lieu den khi mo. Con nao sau khi nau van dong thi bo.

Chapter 5 - Cooking Methods and Presentation
When the assessor asks about a dish, answer with the cooking method, key steps, temperature/doneness, sauce and presentation. Do not give only the name of the dish.
Cooking method guide
Method
Heat type
Best for
Interview tip
Grilling
High direct heat
Steak, fish fillet, chicken breast
Good colour and smoky flavour.
Roasting
Dry heat in oven
Whole chicken, lamb rack, pork loin
Rest meat before carving.
Braising
Slow cooking with liquid
Beef chuck, lamb shoulder, duck leg
For tougher cuts.
Stewing
Small pieces cooked in liquid
Beef stew, lamb stew
Cook until tender.
Poaching
Gentle cooking in liquid
Fish, chicken breast
Keep below boiling to protect texture.
Deep-frying
Hot oil
Crumbed chicken, calamari
Do not overcrowd fryer.
Steaming
Steam heat
Fish, vegetables
Retains moisture.
Presentation principles
Follow the Standard Recipe Card for portion size and plating style.
Use colour contrast: protein, starch, vegetables and garnish.
Make sure the plate is clean and the sauce is not messy.
Garnish must be edible and suitable for the dish.
Do not overload the plate. Keep it balanced and consistent.
Chapter 6 - Dish Process Cards
Grilled Steak
Process
Receive and store beef correctly. Portion steak according to SRC. Season with salt and pepper. Preheat grill. Cook to requested doneness. Rest meat. Plate with sauce and side dishes.
Speaking version
For grilled steak, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Roast Chicken
Process
Thaw in cool room if frozen. Season or marinate. Roast in oven until the centre reaches 75 C. Rest briefly. Portion and plate with gravy and vegetables.
Speaking version
For roast chicken, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Chicken Schnitzel
Process
Flatten chicken evenly. Season. Coat with flour, egg and breadcrumbs. Deep-fry at 160-180 C until golden and cooked through. Drain and serve with lemon and salad.
Speaking version
For chicken schnitzel, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Pan-fried Fish
Process
Check freshness. Pat fish dry. Season lightly. Pan-fry skin-side down first if skin-on. Finish gently to avoid dryness. Serve with lemon butter or herb sauce.
Speaking version
For pan-fried fish, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Steamed Mussels
Process
Clean and debeard. Discard unsafe mussels. Sweat aromatics. Add mussels and liquid. Cover and steam until open. Discard closed mussels.
Speaking version
For steamed mussels, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Beef Pho
Process
Blanch bones to remove impurities. Simmer bones with roasted onion, ginger and spices for several hours. Prepare noodles, sliced beef and herbs. Serve hot broth over the bowl.
Speaking version
For beef pho, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Lamb Rack
Process
Trim and season lamb. Sear for colour. Roast to target doneness. Rest. Slice neatly and serve with jus and vegetables.
Speaking version
For lamb rack, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Pork Belly
Process
Score skin if roasting. Season. Cook slowly until tender. Finish with high heat for crispy skin. Rest and portion.
Speaking version
For pork belly, I follow the Standard Recipe Card. I prepare and store the ingredient safely, choose the correct cooking method, check doneness, then present it with a suitable sauce and garnish.
Chapter 7 - Interview Question Bank
Q1. What meat dishes do you cook?
Model answer: I cook grilled steak, lamb rack, roast pork, beef stew and other dishes depending on the menu. I follow the Standard Recipe Card and check doneness carefully.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q2. How do you choose fresh lamb?
Model answer: I check the colour, smell, texture, packaging, temperature and use-by date. Good lamb should have a fresh smell and firm texture.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q3. What is the best cooking method for lamb shoulder?
Model answer: Braising or slow roasting is best because lamb shoulder is tougher and needs long cooking to become tender.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q4. What is the best cooking method for beef fillet?
Model answer: Grilling or pan-frying is suitable because beef fillet is tender and cooks quickly.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q5. What is the best cooking method for pork belly?
Model answer: Slow roasting or braising is suitable because pork belly needs time to become tender and render fat.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q6. How do you check steak doneness?
Model answer: I use a probe thermometer and also check texture by touch if required. The most accurate method is using the thermometer.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q7. Why is minced meat high risk?
Model answer: Minced meat has more surface area and bacteria can be mixed through the meat, so it must be cooked thoroughly.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q8. What do you do if meat smells bad?
Model answer: I do not use it. I separate it, report to my supervisor, record it and discard or reject it according to procedure.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q9. How do you prevent meat from drying out?
Model answer: I choose the right method, control cooking time and temperature, rest the meat and avoid overcooking.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q10. What poultry dishes do you cook?
Model answer: I cook roast chicken, chicken schnitzel, grilled chicken, chicken stir-fry and braised chicken dishes.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q11. Why is raw chicken dangerous?
Model answer: Raw chicken can contain harmful bacteria such as Salmonella, so I keep it separate and cook it to at least 75 C.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q12. How do you store cooked chicken?
Model answer: I cool it safely, store it covered and labelled in the fridge, and keep it separate from raw food.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q13. Can you refreeze thawed chicken?
Model answer: I do not refreeze chicken if it has been thawed unsafely or left in the danger zone. I follow workplace procedures.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q14. What is duck confit?
Model answer: Duck confit is duck leg cooked slowly in fat until tender, then often crisped before serving.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q15. What sauce goes with duck?
Model answer: Orange sauce, cherry sauce or red wine jus goes well with duck.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q16. What seafood dishes do you cook?
Model answer: I cook grilled fish, pan-fried salmon, steamed mussels, prawn stir-fry and deep-fried calamari.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q17. How do you know seafood is fresh?
Model answer: I check smell, temperature, eyes, gills, flesh firmness and packaging. Fresh seafood has a clean sea smell.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q18. How do you store fresh fish?
Model answer: I store fresh fish at 0-5 C, covered and labelled, usually in the cool room or on ice.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q19. What method is good for delicate fish?
Model answer: Poaching or steaming is good because gentle heat protects the delicate texture.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q20. Why should fish not be boiled strongly?
Model answer: Strong boiling can break the flesh and make fish dry or tough.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q21. What fish is good for deep frying?
Model answer: Lean white fish such as snapper or barramundi can be suitable, especially when battered or crumbed.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q22. How do you avoid cross contamination with seafood?
Model answer: I use separate boards, wash hands, sanitise equipment and keep raw seafood separate from cooked food.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q23. What are shellfish?
Model answer: Shellfish include crustaceans such as prawns, crab and lobster, and molluscs such as mussels, oysters and clams.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q24. How do you cook lobster?
Model answer: Lobster can be boiled, steamed or grilled depending on the recipe. It should be handled safely and cooked carefully to avoid toughness.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q25. What is yield test?
Model answer: A yield test measures usable product after trimming or cooking. It helps control food cost and portion size.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q26. How do you reduce waste when trimming fish?
Model answer: I use good trimmings for fish stock, soup, staff meals or specials if they are safe and suitable.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q27. What are common allergens in this chapter?
Model answer: Fish, shellfish, eggs, dairy, gluten and some sauces may contain allergens, so I always check ingredients.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q28. How do you handle an allergy order?
Model answer: I inform the chef, check ingredients, use clean equipment and avoid cross contamination.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q29. What is the difference between roasting and grilling?
Model answer: Roasting uses dry heat in an oven, while grilling uses strong direct heat from below or above.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q30. How do you organise your station for meat service?
Model answer: I prepare portions, seasonings, sauces, tools and garnishes before service and keep my station clean.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q31. How do you plate a protein dish?
Model answer: I follow the SRC: correct portion, clean plate, suitable sauce, garnish and balanced sides.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q32. What would you do if chicken is still pink after cooking?
Model answer: I continue cooking and check the internal temperature again until it reaches at least 75 C.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q33. What would you do if a fish delivery smells bad?
Model answer: I reject the delivery, record the issue and inform my supervisor.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q34. What should you do with expired meat?
Model answer: I do not use it. I label it for disposal, record it and report to the supervisor.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Q35. Why do you use FIFO for meat and seafood?
Model answer: FIFO ensures older stock is used first, reduces waste and improves food safety.
Vietnamese cue: Tra loi ngan gon, nhac toi an toan thuc pham, nhiet do, FIFO va vi du thuc te.
Chapter 8 - Detailed High-Frequency Ingredient Cards
This chapter gives longer answers for questions that assessors often use to check whether a cook has real kitchen experience. Practise these answers until you can speak naturally without reading.
Question D1: How do you select meat for grilling?
Model Answer
For grilling, I choose tender cuts such as beef fillet, sirloin, rib eye or lamb cutlets. I check the colour, smell, marbling, texture and use-by date. Tender cuts are suitable because grilling is a fast dry-heat method.
Dich tieng Viet
De nuong vi, toi chon phan thit mem nhu beef fillet, sirloin, rib eye hoac lamb cutlets. Toi kiem tra mau, mui, van mo, ket cau va han su dung. Phan thit mem phu hop vi grilling la phuong phap kho va nhanh.

Question D2: How do you select meat for braising?
Model Answer
For braising, I choose tougher cuts such as beef chuck, brisket, lamb shoulder or pork belly. These cuts have more connective tissue and become tender with long, slow cooking in liquid.
Dich tieng Viet
De om, toi chon phan thit dai nhu beef chuck, brisket, vai cuu hoac ba chi heo. Nhung phan nay co nhieu mo lien ket va mem hon khi nau cham voi chat long.

Question D3: What is marinating and why do you marinate meat?
Model Answer
Marinating means soaking meat in a seasoned liquid or paste before cooking. It adds flavour, improves moisture and can help tenderise some cuts. I always marinate food in the fridge, not at room temperature.
Dich tieng Viet
Uop la ngam thit trong chat long hoac hon hop gia vi truoc khi nau. Uop giup tang huong vi, giu am va co the lam mem mot so phan thit. Toi luon uop trong tu mat.

Question D4: What is barding?
Model Answer
Barding means covering lean meat or poultry with fat, usually bacon or fat strips, before cooking. It helps protect the meat from drying out during roasting.
Dich tieng Viet
Barding la phu thit nac hoac gia cam bang mo, thuong la bacon hoac mieng mo, truoc khi nau. No giup thit khong bi kho khi nuong lo.

Question D5: What is larding?
Model Answer
Larding means inserting small strips of fat into lean meat before cooking. It adds moisture and flavour, especially for very lean cuts.
Dich tieng Viet
Larding la chen cac soi mo nho vao thit nac truoc khi nau de tang do am va huong vi.

Question D6: How do you carve roasted meat safely?
Model Answer
I rest the meat first, use a sharp carving knife, stabilise the meat with a carving fork and slice across the grain where possible. I keep my fingers away from the blade.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi de thit nghi truoc, dung dao cat thit sac, co dinh bang nia cat thit va thai ngang tho thit neu phu hop. Toi giu ngon tay tranh xa luoi dao.

Question D7: What is offal?
Model Answer
Offal refers to edible internal organs such as liver, kidney, heart, tongue and brains. It must be very fresh, stored correctly and cooked using a suitable method.
Dich tieng Viet
Offal la noi tang an duoc nhu gan, than, tim, luoi va oc. Phai that tuoi, bao quan dung va nau bang phuong phap phu hop.

Question D8: How do you handle minced meat safely?
Model Answer
I keep minced meat cold, use it quickly and cook it thoroughly. Because bacteria can be mixed through minced meat, it should not be served undercooked.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi giu thit xay lanh, dung nhanh va nau chin ky. Vi vi khuan co the lan ben trong thit xay, khong nen phuc vu chua chin.

Question D9: What is a yield test?
Model Answer
A yield test calculates how much usable product remains after trimming, cooking or portioning. It helps the kitchen control food cost and selling price.
Dich tieng Viet
Yield test tinh luong thuc pham dung duoc con lai sau khi so che, nau hoac chia khau phan. No giup kiem soat chi phi va gia ban.

Question D10: How do you use bones and sinew after trimming meat?
Model Answer
Clean bones and sinew can be used to make stock, jus or sauces if they are fresh and safe. This reduces waste and improves flavour.
Dich tieng Viet
Xuong va gan sach co the dung lam stock, jus hoac sot neu con tuoi va an toan. Viec nay giam lang phi va tang huong vi.

Question D11: How do you prepare a whole chicken for roasting?
Model Answer
I check the chicken quality, pat it dry, season or marinate it, truss it if required, place it on a roasting tray and roast it until the thickest part reaches 75 C.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi kiem tra chat luong ga, lau kho, uop gia vi, buoc ga neu can, dat len khay va nuong den khi phan day nhat dat 75 C.

Question D12: What is trussing?
Model Answer
Trussing means tying poultry with kitchen string so it keeps a neat shape and cooks evenly during roasting.
Dich tieng Viet
Trussing la buoc gia cam bang day bep de giu hinh dang gon va chin deu khi nuong.

Question D13: How do you avoid dry chicken breast?
Model Answer
I control cooking time and temperature, avoid overcooking, rest it briefly, and sometimes use methods such as poaching, marinating or cooking with sauce.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi kiem soat thoi gian va nhiet do, tranh nau qua, de nghi ngan va co the dung phuong phap chan, uop hoac nau voi sot.

Question D14: How do you cook duck breast?
Model Answer
I score the skin, season the breast, start skin-side down in a cool or medium pan to render the fat, then finish cooking gently and rest before slicing.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi khia da, nem gia vi, ap chao mat da truoc o chao khong qua nong de ra bot mo, sau do nau tiep nhe va de nghi truoc khi thai.

Question D15: How is turkey different from chicken when roasting?
Model Answer
Turkey is larger, so it needs longer cooking and careful temperature control. It can dry out, so resting and sometimes basting are important.
Dich tieng Viet
Ga tay lon hon ga thuong nen can nau lau hon va kiem soat nhiet do ky. No de kho nen can de nghi va co the phai tuoi nuoc.

Question D16: What is a suitable method for quail?
Model Answer
Quail is small and tender, so it can be pan-fried, grilled or roasted quickly. It should not be overcooked because it can become dry.
Dich tieng Viet
Chim cut nho va mem nen co the ap chao, nuong vi hoac nuong lo nhanh. Khong nen nau qua vi de bi kho.

Question D17: How do you prepare prawns?
Model Answer
I check freshness, remove the shell if required, devein them, rinse briefly if needed, dry them and cook quickly by grilling, sauteing or stir-frying.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi kiem tra do tuoi, boc vo neu can, lay chi lung, rua nhanh neu can, lau kho va nau nhanh bang nuong, xao ap chao hoac stir-fry.

Question D18: How do you know prawns are cooked?
Model Answer
Cooked prawns change colour, curl slightly and become firm. I avoid overcooking because prawns can become rubbery.
Dich tieng Viet
Tom chin se doi mau, cong nhe va san chac. Toi tranh nau qua vi tom de bi dai nhu cao su.

Question D19: How do you handle oysters safely?
Model Answer
I keep oysters cold, check they are from an approved supplier, discard damaged shells and avoid cross contamination. I use a proper oyster knife and protect my hand when shucking.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi giu hau lanh, kiem tra nha cung cap, bo con vo hong va tranh nhiem cheo. Toi dung dao tach hau dung va bao ve tay khi tach.

Question D20: What is the difference between crustaceans and molluscs?
Model Answer
Crustaceans have shells and jointed legs, such as prawns, crabs and lobsters. Molluscs include mussels, oysters, clams and scallops.
Dich tieng Viet
Crustaceans la giap xac co vo va chan khop nhu tom, cua, tom hum. Molluscs gom trai, hau, ngheu va so diep.

Question D21: How do you prevent overcooking fish?
Model Answer
I choose the correct method, control heat, watch the cooking time and remove the fish when it is just cooked. Fish continues to cook slightly after leaving the heat.
Dich tieng Viet
Toi chon phuong phap dung, kiem soat lua, theo doi thoi gian va lay ca ra khi vua chin. Ca van tiep tuc chin nhe sau khi roi nhiet.

Question D22: Why do you use ice for seafood display or storage?
Model Answer
Ice helps keep seafood at a safe cold temperature and slows spoilage. Seafood must still be protected from contamination and checked regularly.
Dich tieng Viet
Da giup giu hai san lanh an toan va lam cham hu hong. Hai san van phai duoc bao ve khoi nhiem ban va kiem tra thuong xuyen.

Question D23: What is beurre blanc?
Model Answer
Beurre blanc is a warm butter sauce made with reduced white wine, vinegar or lemon juice and butter. It is often served with fish or seafood.
Dich tieng Viet
Beurre blanc la sot bo am lam tu ruou vang trang giam, giam hoac chanh va bo. Thuong dung voi ca hoac hai san.

Question D24: How do you answer if you do not cook a certain ingredient often?
Model Answer
I answer honestly. I can say I do not cook it often, but I understand the basic receiving, storage, preparation and safe cooking principles.

Chapter 10 - Mock Interview and 14-Day Study Plan
Mock Interview: Ingredients Master
Tell me about the meat dishes you cook.
How do you choose fresh beef?
How do you store raw meat safely?
What is the difference between grilling and braising?
How do you cook a medium steak?
Why do you rest meat after cooking?
How do you receive fresh chicken?
Why is chicken high risk?
How do you thaw frozen chicken?
What temperature do you cook poultry to?
How do you crumb chicken?
What poultry dishes do you cook?
How do you receive fresh fish?
How do you know fish is fresh?
How do you fillet a fish?
How do you store seafood?
What sauces go with fish?
How do you handle allergy orders?
How do you reduce waste from meat or fish trimmings?
How do you present a protein dish?
14-Day Study Plan
Day
Task
Day 1
Learn the 6-step ingredient answer formula.
Day 2
Study receiving and storage rules for meat, poultry and seafood.
Day 3
Practise beef/lamb/pork quality indicators and cuts.
Day 4
Practise steak doneness and cooking methods.
Day 5
Practise poultry receiving, thawing and 75 C answer.
Day 6
Practise chicken dish process cards.
Day 7
Mini mock: meat and poultry questions.
Day 8
Study fish freshness indicators and seafood categories.
Day 9
Practise fish cuts and seafood cooking methods.
Day 10
Practise sauces and accompaniments.
Day 11
Revise food safety: cross contamination, allergens, FIFO.
Day 12
Answer 20 interview questions aloud.
Day 13
Full mock interview: record yourself.
Day 14
Review weak answers and practise natural speaking.

Answer Key
Question
Suggested answer
Meat 1
Bright natural colour, clean smell, firm texture, good packaging, correct temperature.
Meat 2
Braising or stewing, because chuck is a tougher cut with connective tissue.
Meat 3
About 60-63 C with a warm pink centre.
Meat 4
To let juices redistribute and keep meat juicy.
Meat 5
Use safe trimmings for stock, sauce, mince, filling or staff meal.
Poultry 1
0-5 C.
Poultry 2
Lowest shelf, covered, labelled and separated from ready-to-eat food.
Poultry 3
In the cool room overnight, not at room temperature.
Poultry 4
At least 75 C in the centre.
Poultry 5
Flour, egg, breadcrumbs, then deep-fry until golden and cooked through.
Seafood 1
Clear eyes, red gills, shiny skin, firm flesh, clean sea smell.
Seafood 2
0-5 C, covered, labelled, on ice or in cool room.
Seafood 3
Crustaceans include prawns/crabs/lobsters; molluscs include mussels/oysters/clams/scallops.
Seafood 4
Two fillets.
Seafood 5
Four fillets.
Self-Scoring Rubric
Score
Meaning
5
Clear, correct, practical answer with temperature or example.
4
Mostly correct answer with minor missing detail.
3
Basic answer but lacks example or safety detail.
2
Partly incorrect or too short.
1
Cannot explain the topic.

Chapter 12 - Reference Notes
This book was rewritten from the user's SA Cook documents and reorganised into a structured interview handbook. Key food safety temperatures were aligned with Australian food safety guidance: potentially hazardous food should be kept at 5 C or colder or 60 C or hotter; poultry and minced/rolled meats should be cooked to at least 75 C in the centre; fresh seafood should be kept at 5 C or colder. Always follow your workplace procedure, local regulations and assessor instructions.

Final Revision Checklist
I can answer the main questions in this book in 45-60 seconds.
I can explain the key procedures without reading.
I know the important vocabulary and can pronounce it clearly.
I can give one real example from my workplace.
I can answer common follow-up questions calmly.

How to use this book
This book is designed for SA Cook interview preparation. Do not memorise every sentence word-for-word. Learn the structure, keywords and safety points, then answer naturally using your real workplace experience.
Step
What to do
Why it matters
1
Read the model answer in English.
Understand the professional answer structure.
2
Read the Vietnamese translation.
Make sure you understand the meaning, not only the words.
3
Learn 5-10 key words per topic.
Assessor wants clear kitchen vocabulary.
4
Practise speaking for 45-60 seconds.
Interview answers should be clear but not too long.
5
Add your own dish examples.
Real examples sound more believable.
Core speaking formula
Situation + Action + Food safety + Quality result. Example: "When I make chicken stock, I start with cold water, simmer gently, skim impurities, cool it quickly and label it before storage. This keeps the stock clear, safe and consistent."

Chapter 1 - Eggs and Egg Dishes
Eggs are common in cook interviews because they test basic cookery, temperature control, emulsification, hygiene and breakfast production.
Topic
Key points to remember
Quality indicators
Clean shell, no cracks, fresh smell, firm white, round yolk.
Storage
Keep refrigerated, away from strong odours, use FIFO.
Food safety
Avoid raw egg risk for vulnerable customers; prevent cross contamination.
Common methods
Boiling, poaching, scrambling, frying, baking, emulsifying.
Classic dishes
Omelette, scrambled eggs, poached eggs, Eggs Benedict, quiche, frittata.
Interview Card 1: How do you check the quality of eggs?
Model Answer
I check that the shells are clean and not cracked. I also check the use-by date and store eggs under refrigeration. When I crack an egg, the white should not be watery and the yolk should be round and firm. If the egg smells unusual, I discard it immediately.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi kiểm tra vỏ trứng sạch, không bị nứt, kiểm tra hạn sử dụng và bảo quản trong tủ mát. Khi đập trứng, lòng trắng không nên quá loãng và lòng đỏ phải tròn, chắc. Nếu trứng có mùi lạ, tôi bỏ ngay.
Word
IPA
Meaning
shell
/ʃel/
vỏ
crack
/kræk/
nứt
yolk
/jəʊk/
lòng đỏ
watery
/ˈwɔːtəri/
loãng
Common follow-up questions
How do you store eggs?
Why are cracked eggs risky?
What dishes do you make with eggs?
Assessor tip
Mention shell condition, smell and refrigeration. This sounds practical and food-safety focused.
Common mistake
Do not say you wash eggs after receiving unless it is your workplace procedure; washing may spread contamination if done incorrectly.
Interview Card 2: How do you make a poached egg?
Model Answer
I bring water to a gentle simmer, add a little vinegar, crack the egg into a small bowl and slide it gently into the water. I cook it for about 3 to 4 minutes until the white is set and the yolk is still soft. I drain it carefully before serving.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi đun nước sôi lăn tăn, thêm một ít giấm, đập trứng vào chén nhỏ rồi nhẹ nhàng thả vào nước. Tôi nấu khoảng 3-4 phút cho lòng trắng chín và lòng đỏ vẫn mềm. Sau đó vớt ra để ráo trước khi phục vụ.
Word
IPA
Meaning
gentle simmer
/ˈdʒentl ˈsɪmə/
sôi lăn tăn
vinegar
/ˈvɪnɪɡə/
giấm
set
/set/
đông lại
drain
/dreɪn/
để ráo
Common follow-up questions
Why do you add vinegar?
How do you avoid breaking the yolk?
What dishes use poached eggs?
Assessor tip
Explain sequence clearly: simmer, vinegar, crack, slide, cook, drain.
Common mistake
Do not say boiling strongly; boiling can break the egg.
Interview Card 3: How do you make scrambled eggs?
Model Answer
I whisk the eggs with a little salt and cream or milk if required by the recipe. I cook them over low heat, stir gently and remove them from the heat before they become dry. The final texture should be soft and creamy.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi đánh trứng với một ít muối và kem hoặc sữa nếu công thức yêu cầu. Tôi nấu trên lửa nhỏ, khuấy nhẹ và lấy ra khỏi bếp trước khi trứng bị khô. Kết cấu cuối cùng phải mềm và béo mịn.
Word
IPA
Meaning
whisk
/wɪsk/
đánh
low heat
/ləʊ hiːt/
lửa nhỏ
creamy
/ˈkriːmi/
mềm béo
texture
/ˈtekstʃə/
kết cấu
Common follow-up questions
Why use low heat?
How do you stop eggs becoming dry?
What garnish can you use?
Assessor tip
Temperature control is the key point. Mention low heat and soft texture.
Common mistake
Do not overcook scrambled eggs; dry scrambled eggs sound unprofessional.
Interview Card 4: What is Eggs Benedict?
Model Answer
Eggs Benedict is a classic breakfast dish. It usually includes a toasted English muffin, ham or bacon, poached eggs and Hollandaise sauce. It should be served immediately because the egg and sauce are best when fresh and warm.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Eggs Benedict là món ăn sáng cổ điển, gồm English muffin nướng, ham hoặc bacon, trứng chần và sốt Hollandaise. Món này nên phục vụ ngay vì trứng và sốt ngon nhất khi còn mới và ấm.
Word
IPA
Meaning
English muffin
/ˈɪŋɡlɪʃ ˈmʌfɪn/
bánh English muffin
poached egg
/pəʊtʃt eɡ/
trứng chần
Hollandaise
/ˌhɒlənˈdeɪz/
sốt Hollandaise
Common follow-up questions
What sauce is used for Eggs Benedict?
How do you make Hollandaise?
How do you plate it?
Assessor tip
This question often connects to Hollandaise, warm emulsion and splitting sauce.
Interview Card 5: What is the difference between frittata and quiche?
Model Answer
A frittata is an egg-based dish cooked without pastry, often finished in the oven. A quiche has a pastry crust and usually contains egg custard with cream, cheese, vegetables or meat.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Frittata là món trứng không có vỏ bánh, thường hoàn thiện trong lò. Quiche có vỏ pastry và nhân custard từ trứng, kem, phô mai, rau hoặc thịt.
Word
IPA
Meaning
pastry crust
/ˈpeɪstri krʌst/
vỏ bánh
custard
/ˈkʌstəd/
hỗn hợp trứng sữa
finish in the oven
/ˈfɪnɪʃ ɪn ði ˈʌvn/
hoàn thiện trong lò
Common follow-up questions
Which one has pastry?
What filling can you use?
How do you avoid overcooking egg custard?
Chapter 2 - Vegetables, Fruits and Farinaceous Dishes
This chapter covers preparation, storage, quality indicators, vegetable cuts and farinaceous foods such as pasta, rice, polenta and couscous.
Food group
Quality indicators
Storage notes
Leafy greens
Crisp leaves, no slime, fresh smell
Keep cold, dry, covered and away from raw meat.
Root vegetables
Firm, no mould, no soft spots
Cool, dry storage or cool room depending on item.
Mushrooms
Firm, dry surface, no dark slime
Keep refrigerated and ventilated.
Fruit
Fresh colour, no bruising or mould
Store according to ripeness and recipe use.
Cooked starches
Fresh smell, correct texture
Cool quickly, label and refrigerate.
Interview Card 6: How do you store fresh vegetables?
Model Answer
I store vegetables in clean containers in the cool room at the correct temperature. I keep them separate from raw meat, poultry and seafood. Leafy vegetables should be kept dry and covered to maintain freshness and reduce contamination risk.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi bảo quản rau trong hộp sạch trong kho mát ở nhiệt độ phù hợp. Tôi tách rau khỏi thịt sống, gia cầm và hải sản. Rau lá nên giữ khô và che đậy để duy trì độ tươi và giảm nguy cơ nhiễm bẩn.
Word
IPA
Meaning
leafy vegetables
/ˈliːfi ˈvedʒtəbəlz/
rau lá
freshness
/ˈfreʃnəs/
độ tươi
contamination risk
/kənˌtæmɪˈneɪʃn rɪsk/
nguy cơ nhiễm bẩn
Common follow-up questions
Why should vegetables be separated from raw meat?
How do you use FIFO with vegetables?
What do you do with spoiled vegetables?
Assessor tip
Mention clean containers, correct temperature, separation and FIFO.
Interview Card 7: What vegetable cuts do you know?
Model Answer
I know julienne, brunoise, batonnet, dice, chiffonade, paysanne and mirepoix. I choose the cut according to the recipe, cooking method and presentation requirements.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi biết các kiểu cắt julienne, brunoise, batonnet, dice, chiffonade, paysanne và mirepoix. Tôi chọn kiểu cắt theo công thức, phương pháp nấu và yêu cầu trình bày.
Word
IPA
Meaning
julienne
/ˌdʒuːliˈen/
thái sợi
brunoise
/bruːˈnwɑːz/
hạt lựu nhỏ
chiffonade
/ˌʃɪfəˈnɑːd/
thái sợi rau lá
mirepoix
/ˈmɪrpwɑː/
rau củ nền
Common follow-up questions
Which cut is used for soup garnish?
Which cut is used for stir-fry?
Why is consistency important?
Assessor tip
Connect cuts to dishes. For example: julienne for stir-fry, brunoise for garnish, mirepoix for stocks.

Interview Card 8: How do you cook pasta al dente?
Model Answer
I boil salted water, add pasta and stir to prevent sticking. I cook it until it is tender but still slightly firm in the centre. Then I drain it and combine it with the sauce immediately or cool it safely if it is for later service.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi đun nước muối sôi, cho pasta vào và khuấy để không dính. Tôi nấu đến khi pasta mềm nhưng vẫn hơi chắc ở giữa. Sau đó để ráo và trộn với sốt ngay hoặc làm nguội an toàn nếu dùng sau.
Word
IPA
Meaning
al dente
/ˌæl ˈdenteɪ/
chín tới, còn độ chắc
drain
/dreɪn/
để ráo
sticking
/ˈstɪkɪŋ/
bị dính
Common follow-up questions
What does al dente mean?
Why do you salt pasta water?
How do you store cooked pasta?
Interview Card 9: How can you reduce vegetable waste?
Model Answer
I use correct knife skills, follow the recipe, trim carefully and use suitable offcuts for stocks, soups, sauces or garnishes. I also follow FIFO and check the quality of vegetables every day.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi dùng kỹ năng dao đúng, làm theo công thức, cắt tỉa cẩn thận và dùng phần cắt còn tốt cho nước dùng, súp, sốt hoặc trang trí. Tôi cũng áp dụng FIFO và kiểm tra chất lượng rau mỗi ngày.
Word
IPA
Meaning
offcuts
/ˈɒfkʌts/
phần cắt còn lại
trim
/trɪm/
cắt tỉa
garnish
/ˈɡɑːnɪʃ/
trang trí
Common follow-up questions
What offcuts can be used in stock?
How do you record waste?
Why is waste control important?
Assessor tip
Waste questions often connect to food cost and sustainability.
Chapter 3 - Salads, Dressings and Sandwiches
Salads test mise en place, freshness, knife skills, dressing knowledge and presentation. Sandwich questions test basic preparation, hygiene and portion control.
Salad component
Meaning
Example
Base
The foundation of the salad
Lettuce, mixed leaves
Body
Main ingredient
Chicken, seafood, eggs, vegetables
Garnish
Edible decoration
Croutons, herbs, Parmesan, radish
Dressing
Sauce that seasons the salad
Vinaigrette, Caesar, mayonnaise-based dressing
Interview Card 10: How many types of salad and dressings do you know?
Model Answer
There are simple salads and mixed or composed salads. Common dressings include vinaigrette, mayonnaise, mayonnaise-based dressings and vinaigrette-based dressings. Vinaigrette is usually made with three parts oil and one part vinegar, then seasoned with salt and pepper.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Có salad đơn giản và salad trộn hoặc salad phối hợp. Các loại dressing phổ biến gồm vinaigrette, mayonnaise, dressing nền mayonnaise và dressing nền vinaigrette. Vinaigrette thường gồm 3 phần dầu và 1 phần giấm, nêm muối và tiêu.
Word
IPA
Meaning
composed salad
/kəmˈpəʊzd ˈsæləd/
salad phối hợp
vinaigrette
/ˌvɪnɪˈɡret/
sốt dầu giấm
mayonnaise-based
/ˌmeɪəˈneɪz beɪst/
nền mayonnaise
Common follow-up questions
What is Caesar dressing?
What are the four parts of a salad?
When do you dress a salad?
Assessor tip
If asked ratios, say 3 parts oil to 1 part vinegar for a basic vinaigrette.
Interview Card 11: How do you make Caesar salad?
Model Answer
I use fresh cos lettuce, crispy bacon, croutons, Parmesan cheese and Caesar dressing. I wash and dry the lettuce, cut it into bite-sized pieces, mix it with dressing just before service and garnish it neatly. Sometimes I add grilled chicken or prawns depending on the menu.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi dùng xà lách cos tươi, bacon giòn, croutons, Parmesan và sốt Caesar. Tôi rửa và làm khô xà lách, cắt vừa ăn, trộn dressing ngay trước khi phục vụ và trang trí gọn gàng. Đôi khi thêm gà nướng hoặc tôm tùy menu.
Word
IPA
Meaning
cos lettuce
/kɒs ˈletɪs/
xà lách cos
croutons
/ˈkruːtɒnz/
bánh mì giòn
bite-sized
/baɪt saɪzd/
vừa ăn
Common follow-up questions
What is in Caesar dressing?
Why do you dry lettuce?
Can you add protein?
Assessor tip
Always mention fresh ingredients, dressing close to service and edible garnish.
Sandwich type
Description
Example filling
Conventional
Two slices of bread with spread and filling
Chicken mayo
Open sandwich
One slice of bread with toppings
Smoked salmon
Club sandwich
Multi-layer sandwich, often cut diagonally
Chicken, bacon, lettuce
Pinwheel
Rolled flatbread, chilled and sliced
Cream cheese and vegetables
Finger sandwich
Small tea sandwich, trimmed and cut neatly
Egg mayo
Interview Card 12: What bread can be used for sandwiches?
Model Answer
Common breads include white bread, wholegrain bread, sourdough, baguette, tortilla, flatbread, naan and rolls. I choose the bread based on the filling, service style and customer preference.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Các loại bánh mì phổ biến gồm white bread, wholegrain, sourdough, baguette, tortilla, flatbread, naan và rolls. Tôi chọn bánh theo nhân, kiểu phục vụ và sở thích khách.
Word
IPA
Meaning
sourdough
/ˈsaʊədəʊ/

Chapter 4 - Stocks
Stocks are foundation items in commercial cookery. They are used for soups, sauces, stews, braises, risotto and many other dishes.
Stock
Main ingredients
Typical cooking note
White stock
Bones, mirepoix, herbs, cold water
Bones are not roasted.
Brown stock
Roasted bones, roasted mirepoix, tomato paste, cold water
Roasting develops colour and flavour.
Chicken stock
Chicken bones, mirepoix, herbs
Simmer gently and skim.
Fish stock
Fish bones, mirepoix, herbs
Short simmer to avoid bitterness.
Vegetable stock
Vegetables, herbs, water
Good for vegetarian dishes.
Interview Card 13: What is a stock and why is it important?
Model Answer
A stock is a flavourful liquid made by simmering bones, vegetables, herbs and water. It is important because it is the base for soups, sauces, stews and braises. A good stock improves the flavour and quality of many dishes.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Stock là chất lỏng có hương vị được hầm từ xương, rau củ, thảo mộc và nước. Nó quan trọng vì là nền của súp, sốt, món hầm và món om. Stock tốt giúp món ăn ngon và chất lượng hơn.
Word
IPA
Meaning
flavourful liquid
/ˈfleɪvəfəl ˈlɪkwɪd/
chất lỏng có hương vị
simmer
/ˈsɪmə/
hầm lăn tăn
base
/beɪs/
nền
Common follow-up questions
What dishes use stock?
How should good stock look?
How do you store stock?
Interview Card 14: How do you make brown beef stock?
Model Answer
I roast beef bones and mirepoix until brown, then place them in a stockpot with cold water, herbs and sometimes tomato paste. I bring it to a simmer, skim impurities and simmer it gently for several hours. After cooking, I strain, cool quickly, label and store it correctly.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi nướng xương bò và mirepoix đến khi nâu, sau đó cho vào nồi với nước lạnh, thảo mộc và đôi khi thêm tomato paste. Tôi đun sôi lăn tăn, hớt bọt và hầm nhẹ trong vài giờ. Sau khi nấu, tôi lọc, làm nguội nhanh, ghi nhãn và bảo quản đúng.
Word
IPA
Meaning
roast bones
/rəʊst bəʊnz/
nướng xương
mirepoix
/ˈmɪrpwɑː/
hỗn hợp hành/cà rốt/cần tây
skim impurities
/skɪm ɪmˈpjʊərətiz/
hớt tạp chất
strain
/streɪn/
lọc
Common follow-up questions
Why start with cold water?
Why simmer instead of boiling?
How do you cool stock safely?
Assessor tip
Say "simmer", not "boil hard". Strong boiling can make stock cloudy.
Interview Card 15: How should a good stock look?
Model Answer
A good stock should be clear, full of flavour, not greasy and not cloudy. It should have a clean aroma and may become slightly gelatinous when chilled, especially if bones with collagen are used.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Stock tốt phải trong, đậm vị, không béo nhiều và không đục. Nó có mùi thơm sạch và có thể hơi đông lại khi lạnh nếu dùng xương có collagen.
Word
IPA
Meaning
clear
/klɪə/
trong
cloudy
/ˈklaʊdi/
đục
greasy
/ˈɡriːsi/
nhiều dầu mỡ
gelatinous
/dʒəˈlætɪnəs/
hơi đông gelatin
Common follow-up questions
What makes stock cloudy?
How do you remove fat?
Why is gelatin a good sign?
Interview Card 16: How do you make fish stock?
Model Answer
I use clean fish bones, mirepoix, herbs and cold water. I simmer it gently for about 20 minutes because fish bones cook quickly and a long cooking time can make the stock bitter. Then I strain and cool it safely.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi dùng xương cá sạch, mirepoix, thảo mộc và nước lạnh. Tôi hầm nhẹ khoảng 20 phút vì xương cá chín nhanh và hầm quá lâu có thể làm stock bị đắng. Sau đó tôi lọc và làm nguội an toàn.
Word
IPA
Meaning
fish bones
/fɪʃ bəʊnz/
xương cá
bitter
/ˈbɪtə/
đắng
cook quickly
/kʊk ˈkwɪkli/
chín nhanh
Common follow-up questions
Why is fish stock cooked for a short time?
What fish bones are suitable?
What dishes use fish stock?
Chapter 5 - Sauces
Sauces add moisture, flavour, colour and balance to dishes. In interviews, sauce questions often test mother sauces, thickening agents, emulsions and how to fix a split sauce.
Mother sauce
Base
Common use
Béchamel
White roux + milk
Lasagne, gratin, white sauce dishes
Velouté
Blond roux + white stock
Chicken or fish sauces
Espagnole
Brown roux + brown stock
Brown sauces, demi-glace base
Tomato
Tomatoes + aromatics
Pasta, braises, pizza base
Hollandaise
Egg yolk + clarified butter
Eggs Benedict, vegetables, fish
Interview Card 17: Name the five mother sauces.
Model Answer
The five classical mother sauces are Béchamel, Velouté, Espagnole, Tomato sauce and Hollandaise. From these sauces, many derivative sauces can be made.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Năm loại sốt mẹ cổ điển gồm Béchamel, Velouté, Espagnole, Tomato sauce và Hollandaise. Từ các sốt này có thể làm nhiều sốt biến thể.
Word
IPA
Meaning
mother sauce
/ˈmʌðə sɔːs/
sốt mẹ
derivative sauce
/dɪˈrɪvətɪv sɔːs/
sốt biến thể
Common follow-up questions
What is Béchamel made from?
What is Velouté made from?
Which sauce is used for Eggs Benedict?
Interview Card 18: What is a roux?
Model Answer
A roux is a cooked mixture of flour and fat, usually butter. It is used as a thickening agent for sauces and soups. Roux can be white, blond or brown depending on cooking time and colour.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Roux là hỗn hợp bột mì và chất béo, thường là bơ, được nấu chín. Nó dùng làm chất làm đặc cho sốt và súp. Roux có thể trắng, vàng hoặc nâu tùy thời gian nấu và màu sắc.
Word
IPA
Meaning
roux
/ruː/
bột bơ làm đặc
thickening agent
/ˈθɪkənɪŋ ˈeɪdʒənt/
chất làm đặc
blond roux
/blɒnd ruː/
roux vàng nhạt
Common follow-up questions
What sauce uses roux?
What is the ratio for roux?
How do you avoid lumps?
Interview Card 19: How do you make Hollandaise sauce?
Model Answer
I whisk egg yolks with a little reduction or lemon juice over gentle heat, then slowly add clarified butter while whisking continuously. The sauce should be smooth, warm and creamy. I control the heat carefully because too much heat can split the sauce.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi đánh lòng đỏ trứng với một ít reduction hoặc nước chanh trên nhiệt nhẹ, rồi thêm bơ trong từ từ trong khi đánh liên tục. Sốt phải mịn, ấm và béo. Tôi kiểm soát nhiệt cẩn thận vì nhiệt quá cao có thể làm sốt bị tách.
Word
IPA
Meaning
egg yolk
/eɡ jəʊk/
lòng đỏ trứng
clarified butter
/ˈklærɪfaɪd ˈbʌtə/
bơ trong
split sauce
/splɪt sɔːs/
sốt bị tách
whisk continuously
/wɪsk kənˈtɪnjuəsli/
đánh liên tục
Common follow-up questions
Why does Hollandaise split?
How can you fix it?
What dishes use Hollandaise?
Assessor tip
Key words: gentle heat, slowly add butter, whisk continuously, smooth and creamy.
Interview Card 20: What is an emulsion?
Model Answer
An emulsion is a mixture of two liquids that normally do not mix, such as oil and vinegar. An emulsifier such as egg yolk helps hold them together. Mayonnaise and Hollandaise are examples of emulsion sauces.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Emulsion là hỗn hợp của hai chất lỏng thường không hòa vào nhau, ví dụ dầu và giấm. Chất nhũ hóa như lòng đỏ trứng giúp chúng kết hợp. Mayonnaise và Hollandaise là ví dụ về sốt nhũ hóa.
Word
IPA
Meaning
emulsion
/ɪˈmʌlʃn/
nhũ tương
emulsifier
/ɪˈmʌlsɪfaɪə/
chất nhũ hóa
oil and vinegar
/ɔɪl ənd ˈvɪnɪɡə/
dầu và giấm
Common follow-up questions
Is mayonnaise a hot or cold emulsion?
What is a warm emulsion?
How do you stop an emulsion from splitting?
Interview Card 21: What are common thickening agents?
Model Answer
Common thickening agents include roux, cornflour, beurre manié, arrowroot, gelatin, agar-agar and reduction. The choice depends on the sauce, texture and dietary requirements.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Các chất làm đặc phổ biến gồm roux, bột bắp, beurre manié, arrowroot, gelatin, agar-agar và reduction. Chọn loại nào phụ thuộc vào sốt, kết cấu và yêu cầu ăn uống.
Word
IPA
Meaning
cornflour
/ˈkɔːnˌflaʊə/
bột bắp
beurre manié
/bɜːr mænˈjeɪ/
bơ trộn bột sống
reduction
/rɪˈdʌkʃn/
cô đặc
Common follow-up questions
Which thickener is gluten-free?

Chapter 6 - Soups
Soup questions test stock knowledge, texture, consistency, reheating and presentation.

Interview Card 22: What are the basic types of soup?
Model Answer
The basic types include clear soups, broths, puree soups, cream soups, bisques, chowders and cold soups. Examples are consommé, chicken broth, pumpkin soup, mushroom cream soup, lobster bisque, clam chowder and gazpacho.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Các loại súp cơ bản gồm súp trong, broth, súp xay, súp kem, bisque, chowder và súp lạnh. Ví dụ: consommé, chicken broth, pumpkin soup, mushroom cream soup, lobster bisque, clam chowder và gazpacho.
Word
IPA
Meaning
consommé
/kənˈsɒmeɪ/
súp trong
bisque
/bɪsk/
súp hải sản đặc

/ˈtʃaʊdə/
súp đặc kiểu chowder
gazpacho
/ɡæzˈpætʃəʊ/
súp lạnh Tây Ban Nha
Common follow-up questions
What is the difference between puree soup and cream soup?

Interview Card 23: How do you make pumpkin soup?
Model Answer
I sweat onion and garlic, add pumpkin and stock, simmer until the pumpkin is soft, then blend the soup until smooth. I adjust seasoning and consistency, reheat it safely and garnish it before service.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Tôi xào nhẹ hành và tỏi, thêm bí đỏ và stock, hầm đến khi bí mềm, rồi xay mịn. Tôi điều chỉnh gia vị và độ đặc, hâm nóng an toàn và trang trí trước khi phục vụ.
Word
IPA
Meaning
sweat onion
/swet ˈʌnjən/
xào nhẹ hành không làm nâu
blend
/blend/
xay
consistency
/kənˈsɪstənsi/
độ đặc
garnish
/ˈɡɑːnɪʃ/
trang trí
Common follow-up questions
What stock can you use?
How do you adjust consistency?
How do you store leftover soup?
Interview Card 24: How do you reconstitute soup before service?
Model Answer
If the soup is stored thick or concentrated, I reheat it safely and adjust the consistency with stock, water or cream depending on the recipe. I bring it to the required temperature, check seasoning and hold it above 60°C if it goes into a bain-marie.
Dịch tiếng Việt
Nếu súp được bảo quản dạng đặc hoặc cô đặc, tôi hâm nóng an toàn và điều chỉnh độ đặc bằng stock, nước hoặc kem tùy công thức. Tôi đưa súp đến nhiệt độ yêu cầu, kiểm tra gia vị và giữ trên 60°C nếu cho vào bain-marie.
Word
IPA
Meaning
reconstitute
/ˌriːˈkɒnstɪtjuːt/
pha/khôi phục lại độ đặc
concentrated
/ˈkɒnsntreɪtɪd/
cô đặc
bain-marie
/ˌbæn məˈriː/
khay giữ nóng
Common follow-up questions
What temperature should hot soup be held at?
How do you avoid burning soup?
How do you cool soup safely?
Assessor tip
Connect this answer to food safety: reheat properly, hold above 60°C, cool leftovers quickly.
Chapter 7 - Production Workflow and Presentation
In a real SA Cook interview, the assessor may ask you to explain the full process from receiving to presenting a dish. Use this workflow.
Stage
What you say in interview
Receiving
Check quality, quantity, temperature, packaging, expiry date.
Storage
Label, date, store at correct temperature, follow FIFO.
Mise en place
Prepare ingredients, tools and equipment before service.
Cooking
Use correct method, temperature and timing.
Holding
Keep hot food above 60°C or cold food 5°C or below where required.
Plating
Use correct portion, colour, garnish and clean plate.
Cleaning
Clean and sanitise workstation after task.
Presentation rule
A finished dish should have correct portion size, balanced colour, suitable garnish, clean plate edge and sauce that complements the main item.

Answer Key
Question
Sample answer
Five egg dishes
Omelette, scrambled eggs, poached eggs, fried eggs, Eggs Benedict, frittata, quiche.
Five mother sauces
Béchamel, Velouté, Espagnole, Tomato, Hollandaise.
Roux
Cooked flour and fat used as a thickening agent.
Simmer stock
To extract flavour gently and keep the stock clear.
Four parts of salad
Base, body, garnish, dressing.
Emulsion
A mixture of liquids that normally do not mix, held together by an emulsifier.
Fish stock
Fish bones, mirepoix, herbs and cold water, simmered briefly.
Al dente
Cooked but still slightly firm in the centre.
Fresh salad
Use fresh dry ingredients, store cold, dress close to service.
Types of soup
Clear, broth, puree, cream, bisque, chowder, cold soup.
